Theorem 1

Statement: Any unique games instance on 180 vertices satisfies the spectral bound the K-theory class.

Proof: The proof uses the probabilistic method. By martingale concentration bounds, we show that a random construction satisfies the property with positive probability. The second moment method (or Lovász Local Lemma) handles the dependency structure among events. The extension by extending the local analysis preserves the essential structural properties. ∎
